GBP BB_MR_20：OOS FILTER 8.49%，BASE 12.24%，相对变化 -3.76 pp；胜率 65.38% 对 BASE 67.65%。 正收益、较小回撤或正收益年份不能替代相对BASE改善；该filter未验证通过。

D-only案例与反例

独立D-only案例原始JSON

以下是独立整理的 2005—2014 观察案例及反例，不用于重新选择候选。joint为两条件交集；A_only/B_only仅满足一项；retained为不拒绝的原交易。收益均是原BASE完整闭合交易的单利和，不是过滤后账户曲线。

USD-direction conflict together with an adverse directional rate spread

Joint losses are present in both halves while A-only and B-only are profitable in both. The combined D single-state A/B totals are positive, so this is a descriptive example of a joint loss pocket hidden by single-state aggregation. The price-only PnL does not credit carry; the explanation is a hypothesis about market context, not financing or causation.

Moderate-low path efficiency with slightly contracting short-horizon volatility

The joint cohort has worse expectancy than A-only and B-only in both halves. B-only is already losing in both halves, so this refines an existing single-state weakness rather than proving that the single-state view missed all losses. The D2 payoff ratio of the joint exceeds A-only/B-only despite lower win rate; exclusion need not improve every metric.

Strong recent directional move inside a low-efficiency 63-quote path

A strong recent move may be an oscillation within a choppy longer path. Joint expectancy is below A-only/B-only in both halves, but B-only also loses; this is a conditional severity distinction. Both halves have modest samples and later performance is unexamined in this explanatory analysis.

反例：intuitive_mechanism_not_supported ·

No efficiency state 0/1 ? volatility-ratio state 3 cell satisfies D1 and D2 n>=10 and negative sums plus combined D negative. No efficiency state 2/3 ? directional-return state 0 cell satisfies those conditions either. This does not prove those mechanisms absent; the predeclared sample/repetition condition is unmet.

没有符合本轮冻结协议的记录。

解释边界与重叠诊断
  • No p-values, causal effect or expected OOS profitability is claimed.
  • The 267 cells reuse previously observed IS and share trades; they are not independent experiments.
  • UNKNOWN is a separate retained partition, never implicitly rejected. A_all/B_all include opposite-feature UNKNOWN and can overlap UNKNOWN partition.
  • All reported PnL is the sum of original complete BASE trade returns, fixed-principal percentage points with zero cost; no filtered daily curve has been simulated by this probe.
  • Efficiency uses absolute 63-quote net price change divided by sum of absolute daily price changes; directional return20 is divided by realized_vol20*sqrt(20/252).
  • DXY contains some studied currencies, so its relation to individual FX is partly structural; directional interest spread is context only and no carry is credited.
  • D totals can differ from D1+D2 due to trades crossing the 2009/2010 boundary.
